## Land, Housing & Property

*Total Scenarios in this category: 110*

| ID | Subcategory | Scenario Title | Core Problem / Friction | Critical Checklist | Primary Risk |
| :---: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | Land Purchase | **Buying agricultural land with crop-tenant (Borga) history** | Tenants may have protected statutory tenancy rights preventing eviction. | Tenant relinquishment deed, land revenue records, physical survey | Loss of physical possession |
| 2 | Land Purchase | **Buying residential plot in an unapproved private housing scheme** | Private housing projects must have environmental and development authority clearances. | RAJUK/CDA approval, layout plan clearance, wetland classification check | Demolition or refusal of utility connections |
| 3 | Land Purchase | **Buying land through a registered Power of Attorney (PoA)** | PoA automatically terminates if the principal dies before deed execution. | Verify principal is alive, verify PoA registration at sub-registry, check specific sale authorization | Void transaction and total loss of money |
| 4 | Land Purchase | **Buying ancestral land from one brother without other co-sharers' consent** | A co-sharer cannot sell a specific demarcated portion without mutual partition (Bonton-nama). | Registered Partition Deed (আপোষ বন্টননামা), joint survey, written NOC from all co-sharers | Co-sharers filing pre-emption suit (হকশুফা) |
| 5 | Land Purchase | **Buying land bordering river or water bodies (Char land)** | Alluvion and diluvion laws (পয়স্তি ও শিকস্তি) often vest eroded or newly formed land back to the government. | Check Diara survey, AC Land clearance, riverbank demarcation records | Government reclamation as Khas land |
| 6 | Land Purchase | **Buying land under pending Civil Court suit (Lis Pendens)** | Section 52 of Transfer of Property Act voids transfers during ongoing property lawsuits. | Check District Judge court records, Sub-registry search certificate for encumbrances | Deed declared null and void by court |
| 7 | Land Purchase | **Buying roadside commercial plot with potential road widening plans** | Roads & Highways Department (RHD) may have notified acquisition line. | RHD master plan check, acquisition notice verification at DC office | Land acquisition without compensation for illegal structures |
| 8 | Land Purchase | **Buying hill or sloping land in hilly terrain** | Strict environmental bans on cutting hills exist under Environment Conservation Act. | DoE (Department of Environment) NOC, Soil stability certificate | Criminal prosecution for illegal hill cutting |
| 9 | Land Purchase | **Buying industrial land within an export processing or economic zone buffer** | BEZA or BEPZA buffer zones impose strict usage and zoning restrictions. | BEZA zoning master plan, Industrial clearance NOC, environmental categorization | Inability to obtain factory license or gas/power |
| 10 | Land Purchase | **Buying tea garden or rubber plantation grant land** | Tea estate leases are specialized government leases that prohibit sub-division or residential conversion. | Tea Board approval, Ministry of Land clearance, original lease terms | Immediate lease cancellation and state repossession |
| 11 | Apartment Purchase | **Buying an under-construction flat from a private developer** | Developers often delay projects for years and hide structural deviations. | Developer REHAB membership, RAJUK/CDA approved sheet, milestone payment schedule, penalty clause | Stuck life savings, delayed possession by 5+ years |
| 12 | Apartment Purchase | **Buying a ready flat from a first-generation owner (Second-hand flat)** | The seller must transfer both the apartment and the undivided proportion of land share. | Original registered deed, flat mutation in AC Land office, building association NOC, utility clearance | Cannot register or mutate flat ownership |
| 13 | Apartment Purchase | **Buying a flat on the top floor with exclusive roof rights** | Roof access is by default a common facility for all owners unless explicitly registered in the master deed. | Master deed wording, building bylaws, building association resolution | Lawsuits from neighbors and loss of roof access |
| 14 | Apartment Purchase | **Buying an apartment where developer mortgaged project land to a bank** | If the developer defaults on project loan, the bank can auction the entire building including flats. | Bank Non-Encumbrance Certificate (NEC), tripartite agreement, redemption deed | Bank auctioning your home |
| 15 | Apartment Purchase | **Buying an apartment with basement car parking space** | Car parking spaces must be specifically numbered and demarcated in the sale deed. | Parking allotment letter, layout diagram attached to deed, association registry | Neighbor disputes and inability to park |
| 16 | Apartment Purchase | **Buying a studio apartment in a mixed commercial-residential building** | Commercial utility tariffs and visitor regulations may disrupt residential living. | Approved building usage certificate, separate utility meters, noise zoning | Exorbitant commercial electricity and gas bills |
| 17 | Apartment Purchase | **Buying a flat with unauthorized cantilever/balcony extensions** | Deviations from approved architectural plans violate building codes. | Compare actual built dimensions against RAJUK/CDA structural drawing | Municipal notice and forced demolition of extensions |
| 18 | Apartment Purchase | **Buying an apartment in a cooperative housing society** | Cooperative bylaws require society committee approval before any transfer. | Society managing committee NOC, share certificate transfer, audit clearance | Society refusing recognition or maintenance access |
| 19 | Apartment Purchase | **Buying a duplex apartment created by joining two floors** | Internal staircase modifications often compromise building structural integrity. | Structural engineer vetting, fire safety clearance, separate deeds for both units | Building collapse hazard or fire authority sealing |
| 20 | Apartment Purchase | **Purchasing an apartment without an Occupancy Certificate (OC)** | Without an OC, a building is legally deemed unfit for human habitation. | City Corporation / Development Authority Occupancy Certificate | Utility disconnection and eviction notice |
| 21 | Revenue & Records | **Applying for e-Mutation (ই-নামজারি) online** | The official fee is fixed (approx 1,170 BDT), yet people pay thousands to middlemen. | mutation.land.gov.bd account, scanned deed, via-dalil, up-to-date khajna | Extortion by touts and rejection due to missing via-deeds |
| 22 | Revenue & Records | **Tracing chain of deeds (বায়া দলিল / Via-Dalil) for 30 years** | A single missing link in deed transfers voids legal ownership chain. | Sub-registry index search, non-encumbrance certificate (NEC), record room copies | Fraudulent title discovered after full payment |
| 23 | Revenue & Records | **Reconciling CS, SA, RS, and BS/City Survey Khatians** | Discrepancies in plot numbers or owner names across historical surveys indicate fraud or dispute. | Comparative Khatian ledger chart, Mouza map tracing, field verification | Buying someone else's land due to surveyor error |
| 24 | Revenue & Records | **Paying Land Development Tax (e-Khajna) online** | Holding numbers must be linked to NID and digitized before tax can be deposited. | ldtax.gov.bd registration, holding entry, digital Dakhila download | Accumulated interest penalties and auction for arrears |
| 25 | Revenue & Records | **Verifying certified copy of Khatian from DC Record Room** | Fake stamped parchhas are commonly fabricated by local brokers. | DC office QR code / barcode verification, volume ledger comparison | Purchasing forged land documents |
| 26 | Revenue & Records | **Correcting clerical errors in BS Khatian (Rev. Case / Section 31)** | Incorrect names or shares in BS Khatian can only be corrected through Land Survey Tribunal or Assistant Commissioner (Land). | Civil suit or Section 31 application, original title deeds, historical records | Perpetual title cloud preventing sale |
| 27 | Revenue & Records | **Handling land listed under Vested Property (অর্পিত সম্পত্তি) Ka / Kha list** | Buying Ka-listed vested property is strictly illegal and automatically vests in the government. | Check Upazila Nirbahi Officer (UNO) / AC Land Vested Property Gazette register | Instant forfeiture of land to government |
| 28 | Revenue & Records | **Verifying Waqf property status before transaction** | Waqf properties cannot be sold or mortgaged without Administrator of Waqfs Bangladesh sanction. | Waqf Administrator office verification, Waqf enrollment register | Deed declared null and void under Waqf Ordinance |
| 29 | Revenue & Records | **Checking Section 4 Acquisition Notice on land** | Once Section 4 notice is issued by DC office, any sale or construction is void. | District Commissioner Land Acquisition (LA) Branch register search | Loss of money; compensation paid only to recorded owner |
| 30 | Revenue & Records | **Demarcating physical boundary using digital GPS and Mouza sheet** | Paper land area often does not match ground reality due to encroachment. | Independent government-certified Amin, 16-inch=1-mile Mouza map, total station survey | Receiving less land than paid for |
| 31 | Tenancy & Rental | **Drafting a legally enforceable residential tenancy agreement** | Standard stationary shop agreements often lack vital termination, maintenance, and dispute clauses. | Stamp paper agreement, security deposit refund terms, annual escalation cap | Arbitrary eviction or lost deposit |
| 32 | Tenancy & Rental | **Filing police tenant verification form (CIMS)** | Failing to register tenants with the local police station is a punishable offense under police regulations. | Citizen Information Management System (CIMS) form, tenant NID, passport photos | Police detention or legal penalties during security raids |
| 33 | Tenancy & Rental | **Recovering security deposit from an abusive landlord** | Landlords frequently invent fake damage to deduct entire security deposits. | Initial inventory checklist with dated photos, written move-out notice, legal notice | Forfeiture of 2-6 months' rent deposit |
| 34 | Tenancy & Rental | **Evicting a commercial tenant who stopped paying rent** | You cannot physically lock a tenant out without court order without committing criminal trespass. | Notice to Quit under Transfer of Property Act, suit for eviction in Small Causes Court | Months of lost rent and criminal counter-charges |
| 35 | Tenancy & Rental | **Renting to bachelor students / professionals** | Building housing societies often enforce arbitrary, illegal bachelor bans. | Building society bylaws review, owner agreement with society permission clause | Sudden harassment and forced midnight eviction |
| 36 | Tenancy & Rental | **Renting commercial space for a restaurant or bakery** | Requires high-voltage power sanction, commercial gas line, and Fire Service clearance. | Building commercial conversion certificate, load feasibility from electric utility | Inability to open business after paying hefty advance |
| 37 | Tenancy & Rental | **Sub-letting rooms or commercial space** | Sub-letting without explicit written consent from the landlord violates tenancy law and invites instant eviction. | Written sub-lease consent clause, separate sub-metering agreement | Immediate cancellation of lease and loss of business goodwill |
| 38 | Tenancy & Rental | **Landlord abruptly shutting off water/electricity to force rent hike** | Disconnection of essential services by landlord is an offense under Premises Rent Control Act. | File complaint with Rent Controller (Senior Assistant Judge Court), police GD | Living in uninhabitable conditions |
| 39 | Tenancy & Rental | **Drafting an escalation clause for a 10-year commercial lease** | Uncapped inflation adjustments or vague renewal terms lead to expensive litigation. | Fixed percentage escalation every 3 years, arbitration clause, registered lease deed | Sudden 100% rent demand or forced relocation |
| 40 | Tenancy & Rental | **Renting a warehouse / godown for hazardous or chemical materials** | Requires Department of Explosives permit and specialized industrial zoning. | Explosives license, Fire safety clearance, environmental category clearance | Warehouse raid, heavy fines, or catastrophic fire liability |
| 41 | Construction | **Conducting geotechnical soil test before multi-story building** | Soft clay or organic peat without proper pile foundation leads to building tilt or collapse. | Minimum 3-5 boreholes, SPT (Standard Penetration Test), certified soil report | Foundation failure and building condemnation |
| 42 | Construction | **Obtaining RAJUK / CDA / KDA building plan sanction** | Deviations from setback rules (front, rear, side space) result in construction stoppage. | FAR (Floor Area Ratio) calculation, structural design vetted by certified engineer | Demolition order or heavy compounding fines |
| 43 | Construction | **Hiring a general contractor vs labor contract with self-procured materials** | Contractors often substitute Grade 60 rod with Grade 40 and use adulterated cement. | Itemized bill of quantities (BOQ), batch testing of rebar and concrete cylinders | Compromised structural strength and budget overrun |
| 44 | Construction | **Installing rooftop solar net-metering system** | Requires distribution utility inspection and bi-directional meter installation. | Utility net-metering policy clearance, solar panel tier-1 certification, inverter safety | Power rejection or dangerous electrical backfeed |
| 45 | Construction | **Sizing and installing an RCC septic tank and soak well** | Inadequate septic capacity leads to sewage backup and contamination of ground water. | Percolation test, multi-chamber design, water table assessment | Foul odor, continuous overflow, health department fines |
| 46 | Construction | **Waterproofing an exposed concrete roof slab** | Traditional lime terracing often cracks; polyurethane or elastomeric membrane required. | Surface preparation, primer coat, fiber-mesh reinforcement, flood ponding test | Ceiling seepage, ruined plaster, corroded rebar |
| 47 | Construction | **Installing a passenger elevator/lift in a private building** | Requires Electric Inspector to the Government of Bangladesh (OIEG) safety clearance. | Elevator load test certificate, annual maintenance contract (AMC), emergency rescue device (ARD) | Passenger entrapment accidents and legal liability |
| 48 | Construction | **Designing fire exit staircase according to BNBC (National Building Code)** | Fire doors must have minimum 2-hour fire rating with positive pressure ventilation. | Bangladesh National Building Code compliance, Fire Service NOC, panic bars | Building sealed by Fire Service or fatal entrapment |
| 49 | Construction | **Deep tube-well boring permission in urban areas** | Unauthorized deep tube-wells are strictly banned by WASA to prevent aquifer depletion. | WASA clearance certificate, water quality testing (Arsenic, Salinity, Coliform) | Borehole sealing and confiscation of pump equipment |
| 50 | Construction | **Demolishing an old dangerous building safely** | Demolition requires municipal permission and vibration monitoring for adjacent buildings. | Municipal demolition permit, third-party structural survey of neighbors, safety netting | Collapse on neighboring structures and manslaughter charges |
| 51 | Disputes & Law | **Filing a Pre-emption Suit (হকশুফা মোকদ্দমা) against third-party buyer** | Co-sharers have legal priority to buy adjacent ancestral land within statutory deadline. | File suit within statutory limitation under State Acquisition and Tenancy Act, deposit purchase price | Permanent loss of contiguous family land |
| 52 | Disputes & Law | **Defending against forged deed (জাল দলিল) created by land grabbers** | Filing a suit under Specific Relief Act for cancellation of fraudulent deed. | Deed cancellation suit (Section 39 SR Act), handwriting expert verification, Sub-registry volume audit | Loss of legal title to fraudsters |
| 53 | Disputes & Law | **Removing an illegal boundary wall built by encroaching neighbor** | Directly destroying the wall leads to criminal mischief charges; legal demarcation required. | Civil court demarcation suit, temporary injunction (Order 39 CPC), police assistance | Violent physical altercation or reverse criminal case |
| 54 | Disputes & Law | **Handling emergency Section 144 / 145 CrPC notice on land** | Executive Magistrate can impose status quo to prevent imminent breach of peace over land. | Appear before Executive Magistrate with title documents, seek police report | Arrest for violating prohibitory orders |
| 55 | Disputes & Law | **Distributing property of a deceased parent among sons and daughters** | Muslim or Hindu personal succession law governs exact share calculations. | Obtain Waris / Succession certificate from City Corporation / Union Parishad, registered Bonton-nama | Lifelong family feud and frozen land transactions |
| 56 | Disputes & Law | **Cancelling an irrevocable Power of Attorney (অপ্রত্যাহারযোগ্য আমমোক্তারনামা)** | Requires formal registered cancellation deed or civil court declaration. | Public notice in daily newspaper, registered notice to attorney, Sub-registry notification | Attorney selling land behind your back |
| 57 | Disputes & Law | **Filing Land Survey Tribunal (LST) suit for BS survey errors** | LST is the exclusive forum for challenging incorrect BS survey records. | Suit within limitation period, verified CS/SA/RS chain, witness affidavits | Records permanently fixed in stranger's name |
| 58 | Disputes & Law | **Resolving mutation appeal before Additional Deputy Commissioner (Revenue)** | When AC Land rejects legitimate mutation or approves illegal mutation for rivals. | ADC Revenue appeal within 30 days, certified order sheet, hearing representation | Title records remaining frozen |
| 59 | Disputes & Law | **Recovering possession under Section 9 of Specific Relief Act** | Dispossessed without consent can reclaim possession within 6 months irrespective of title. | File Section 9 suit within 6 months of dispossession, proof of previous possession | Permanent dispossession requiring 10-year title suit |
| 60 | Disputes & Law | **Executing a mutual land exchange deed (বদল দলিল)** | Swapping plots with a neighbor requires formal valuation, stamp duty, and registration. | Equivalent valuation assessment, Sub-registry deed registration, dual mutation | Tax fraud charges or one party reneging |
| 61 | Specialized Land | **Buying property from an expatriate living in UK/USA** | Expatriate Power of Attorney must be executed at Bangladesh Embassy and attested by MOFA. | Embassy execution seal, MOFA attestation, DC office verification within 4 months | Deed rejected by Sub-registrar as forged |
| 62 | Specialized Land | **Buying flat developed under joint venture land-sharing agreement** | Verify owner's allocation vs developer's allocation in the registered joint venture deed. | Registered Joint Venture Agreement, Power of Attorney from landowner to builder | Buying a flat that legally belongs to landowner, not builder |
| 63 | Specialized Land | **Buying an abandoned property (পরিত্যক্ত সম্পত্তি) released by court** | Court of Settlement releases require Gazette notification and Ministry of Housing clearance. | Ministry Gazette notification, Court of Settlement decree, physical inspection | Eviction by Ministry of Housing eviction magistrate |

| 65 | Specialized Land | **Converting agricultural land for industrial factory setup** | Requires land class change (শ্রেণী পরিবর্তন) permission from DC and Ministry of Land. | Department of Agricultural Extension NOC, AC Land inspection, DC conversion order | Factory closure, electricity disconnection, criminal penalty |
| 66 | Specialized Land | **Buying property belonging to a minor (child under 18)** | Parents cannot sell minor's property without District Judge's Court guardianship permission. | Guardianship Court permission order, proof of legal necessity for minor's welfare | Sale declared void once the child turns 18 |
| 67 | Specialized Land | **Buying property from a registered Society, Trust or NGO** | Requires Trust Deed authorization, NGO Affairs Bureau NOC, and board resolution. | Trust Deed powers review, NGOAB approval letter, executive committee resolution | Deed declared ultra vires and cancelled |
| 68 | Specialized Land | **Purchasing land adjacent to railway lines or highway bypass** | Railway and Roads authorities maintain strict right-of-way buffer zones. | Railway estate officer clearance, buffer zone measurement from center-line | Demolition during railway expansion without compensation |
| 69 | Specialized Land | **Buying flat built on leased government land (e.g. RAJUK / CDA plot)** | Transfer of leased plot requires formal permission and transfer fee payment to authority. | Authority permission letter, transfer fee challan, revised lease deed | Registration rejected, flat sealed by development authority |
| 70 | Specialized Land | **Constructing a boundary wall in an active conflict zone** | Requires magistrate presence or police deputation if criminal threats exist. | Executive magistrate order, police deployment challan, certified Amin survey | Violent assault or criminal charges for land grab |
| 71 | Society & Living | **Setting up a registered Apartment Owners Association** | Without a registered society, collecting service charges or repairing common assets is legally unenforceable. | Society registration under Societies Registration Act, drafting bylaws, bank account setup | Rogue owners refusing service charges without recourse |

## Consumer Tech, Hardware & Gadgets

*Total Scenarios in this category: 142*

| ID | Subcategory | Scenario Title | Core Problem / Friction | Critical Checklist | Primary Risk |

| 266 | PC Hardware | **Detecting a used cryptocurrency-mined Graphics Card (GPU)** | Mining GPUs suffer from degraded VRAM solder joints, dry thermal pads, and modified BIOS. | Run FurMark + 3DMark Time Spy for 30 mins, check fan bearing wobble, check VRAM temperature | Artifacts, black screens, and GPU death within weeks |
| 267 | PC Hardware | **Verifying AMD Ryzen AM4/AM5 CPU pins and socket integrity** | Bent or bridged CPU pins prevent memory dual-channel operation or cause boot loops. | Magnifying glass inspection of PGA pins / LGA socket springs, thermal paste overflow check | Fried motherboard socket or dead memory channel |
| 268 | PC Hardware | **Detecting fake Kingston / Samsung USB Flash Drives & MicroSD cards** | Hacked firmware reports 512GB capacity while actual NAND chip is only 16GB, silently overwriting data. | Run H2testw or ValiDrive full write/verify test to confirm true capacity | Catastrophic loss of critical family photos or work files |
| 269 | PC Hardware | **Choosing Power Supply Unit (PSU) and avoiding group-regulated fire hazards** | Cheap uncertified PSUs lack Over-Current (OCP) and Over-Voltage (OVP) protection. | Check Cultists Network PSU Tier List (minimum Tier C), verify 80-Plus Bronze/Gold certification | PSU exploding and frying CPU, GPU, and motherboard |
| 270 | PC Hardware | **Checking NVMe SSD health and preventing fake Chinese clones** | Fake Samsung 980 Pro SSDs use cheap slow SATA controllers with fake controller labels. | Samsung Magician software genuine check, CrystalDiskInfo SMART read/write TBW telemetry | Massive data corruption and abysmal 30 MB/s write speeds |
| 271 | PC Hardware | **Identifying CPU & GPU thermal throttling and repasting** | Dried thermal paste causes CPU to hit 95°C and downclock speeds by 40%. | HWiNFO64 temperature logs under Cinebench load, apply quality non-conductive paste (e.g. Arctic MX-4) | Permanent silicon degradation and sluggish performance |
| 272 | PC Hardware | **Balancing RAM speed, CAS Latency, and Dual-Channel configuration** | Placing RAM in adjacent slots instead of alternating slots drops memory bandwidth by 50%. | Install in slots 2 and 4 (A2/B2), enable XMP/EXPO profile in BIOS, verify CPU-Z memory tab | System running at half memory performance |
| 273 | PC Hardware | **Calculating PC bottleneck between high-end GPU and weak CPU** | Pairing an RTX 4070 with an old 4-core i5 causes stuttering and 40% GPU underutilization. | Monitor GPU utilization percentage in games; if GPU is under 90% while CPU is at 100%, bottleneck exists | Wasting money on expensive GPU without framerate gain |
| 274 | PC Hardware | **Fixing Motherboard BIOS flashback and CPU compatibility** | Newer generation CPUs will not boot on older chipset motherboards without prior BIOS update. | Check motherboard USB BIOS Flashback button, format FAT32 USB drive with latest BIOS file | Black screen on power-on with zero display |
| 275 | PC Hardware | **Diagnosing PC random rebooting / Kernel-Power Event 41** | Often caused by faulty PSU transient spikes, unstable RAM timings, or bad motherboard capacitors. | MemTest86 overnight test, OCCT power supply stress test, Windows Event Viewer log analysis | System crashing during unsaved client presentations |
| 276 | Laptops | **Inspecting a used / refurbished business laptop (ThinkPad / Latitude / EliteBook)** | Corporate lease returns often have Computrace/Absolute software locks or locked BIOS supervisor passwords. | Enter BIOS setup to verify no supervisor password is set, check Computrace status is 'Disabled' | Permanently locked motherboard that cannot be reprogrammed |
| 277 | Laptops | **Checking MacBook battery cycle count and Activation Lock before buying used** | Stolen or iCloud-locked MacBooks will become unusable bricks as soon as you reset them. | Verify Apple ID is logged out, factory reset MacBook, check System Information > Cycle Count (<500) | Holding a 1,000 USD aluminum paperweight |
| 278 | Laptops | **Diagnosing laptop battery wear level on Windows** | Sellers reset battery calibration to hide dying cells. | Run 'powercfg /batteryreport' in command prompt; compare Full Charge Capacity vs Design Capacity | Battery dying within 30 minutes off charger |
| 279 | Laptops | **Testing used gaming laptop GPU TGP (Total Graphics Power) limits** | A 75W RTX 4060 performs significantly worse than a full-power 140W RTX 4060. | Check Nvidia Control Panel > System Info for maximum graphics power wattage | Paying for high-tier GPU label with low-tier performance |
| 280 | Laptops | **Inspecting laptop IPS screen for backlight bleed, dead pixels, and PWM flicker** | Cheap budget laptop screens have severe color banding (45% NTSC) causing extreme eye strain. | Dead pixel test website in dark room, 100% sRGB color gamut confirmation, camera shutter flicker test | Terrible color accuracy for photo/video editing and eye fatigue |

| 283 | Laptops | **Detecting USB-C port failure and loose power delivery pins** | Damaged Type-C charging ports require micro-soldering repairs on mainboard. | Wiggle USB-C charging cable gently; verify continuous charging and data transfer speeds | Laptop suddenly refusing to charge |

| 285 | Smartphones | **Checking iPhone MDM (Mobile Device Management) profile bypass** | Company-owned iPhones bypassed via software will lock permanently upon iOS update or factory reset. | Perform full 'Erase All Content and Settings' in front of seller; set up to home screen without Wi-Fi profile | Phone locked to foreign corporation after reset |

| 288 | Smartphones | **Inspecting smartphone battery health on Android without root** | Android settings often do not display true battery degradation. | Use AccuBattery or Shizuku/aBattery to read battery design capacity vs estimated actual capacity | Phone shutting down abruptly at 20% battery |

| 342 | Tools & Hardware | **Brushless vs Brushed electric motor tools longevity** | Brushed motors wear carbon brushes every 100 hours, spark, and overheat quickly. | Choose Brushless motors for 30% longer runtime, zero maintenance, and cooler operation | Tool burning out in the middle of home renovation |
| 343 | Tools & Hardware | **Selecting wall anchors: Rawlplug vs Hollow Wall Butterfly vs Anchor Bolts** | Using plastic plugs in hollow gypsum or drywall causes heavy mirrors and shelves to crash. | Use spring toggle bolts for drywall; metal expansion sleeve anchor bolts for solid concrete | Heavy TV or shelf crashing onto child or floor |
| 344 | Tools & Hardware | **Angle Grinder Safety: Preventing fatal kickback and wheel shatter** | Using a cracked or unrated abrasive disc causes disc explosion at 11,000 RPM like shrapnel. | Never remove safety wheel guard, wear polycarbonate face shield, check disc RPM rating | Fatal shrapnel injuries to face and throat |
| 345 | Tools & Hardware | **Diagnosing house electrical earth leakage with multimeter** | Missing grounding wire gives mild electric shocks on refrigerator handles and PC cabinets. | Measure AC voltage between Neutral and Earth (should be under 2V; if > 10V, earth is floating) | Electrocution hazard during wet monsoon season |
| 346 | Tools & Hardware | **Unclogging severe bathroom drain pipes without melting PVC with acid** | Pouring concentrated sulfuric acid into drain melts PVC pipe joints, leaking into lower apartment. | Use mechanical plumbing drain auger snake or caustic soda with boiling water, not acid | Pipes melting inside slab, costing 50,000+ BDT in ceiling repairs |
| 554 | Kitchen | **Cast Iron Skillet seasoning and preventing rust** | Washing cast iron with harsh dish soap and leaving it wet dissolves seasoning and causes rust. | Dry immediately on stove heat, wipe with thin coat of high-smoke-point oil (flaxseed/canola) | Black rusty pan flaking metallic rust into food |
| 555 | Kitchen | **Stainless Steel Tri-Ply Cookware: The water drop mercury test** | Food sticks aggressively to stainless steel pans if they are not preheated to Leidenfrost temperature. | Preheat dry pan until water drops bead up and skate like mercury balls before adding oil | Eggs and meat tearing and gluing permanently to bottom of pan |
| 556 | Kitchen | **Chef Knife selection: Japanese VG-10 vs German Carbon Steel** | Hard brittle Japanese knives (60+ HRC) chip and crack if used to hack through bone or frozen food. | Use flexible German steel (X50CrMoV15) for heavy butchering; thin Japanese steel for fine slicing | Chipping a 150 USD Damascus blade on chicken bone |
| 557 | Kitchen | **Gas Stove Burner cleaning: Unclogging yellow lazy flames** | Yellow soot-producing flames mean clogged brass burner jets and dangerous carbon monoxide emission. | Boil brass burners in vinegar-baking soda solution; clear jet holes with thin copper wire | Black soot coating outside of all pots; high gas wastage |
| 558 | Kitchen | **Dishwasher installation: Water hardness and rinse aid settings** | South Asian hard water leaves cloudy white calcium spots and etched glassware. | Set internal water softener salt valve according to municipal water hardness; use finish rinse aid | Cloudy stained glasses and food residue baked onto plates |
| 559 | Home Maintenance | **Fixing Running Toilet Flapper valve and silent water leak** | A warped rubber flapper valve silently leaks 200 liters of water per day into the bowl. | Add 5 drops of food coloring into toilet tank; if color appears in bowl without flushing, replace flapper | Massive water pump electricity bills and running out of overhead water |
| 560 | Home Maintenance | **Sealing bathroom silicone grout against black mold (Aspergillus)** | Standard silicone caulk turns black with toxic fungal mold within 6 months in humid bathrooms. | Scrape old caulk completely, clean with isopropyl alcohol, apply 100% anti-fungal sanitary silicone | Disgusting black mold smelling musty and causing respiratory allergies |
| 561 | Home Maintenance | **Painting Walls: Primer sealer coat vs 2 coats of topcoat paint** | Painting directly on bare plaster without an acrylic primer sealer causes paint to bubble and peel. | Apply alkali-resistant exterior/interior wall primer, sand smooth with 120-grit, apply 2 coats | Sheets of expensive paint peeling off like sunburned skin |
| 562 | Home Maintenance | **Fixing squeaky door hinges with white lithium grease vs WD-40** | WD-40 is a solvent that cleans grease; using it on hinges makes them squeak worse after 3 days. | Clean pin with WD-40, then coat with persistent heavy White Lithium Grease or silicone lubricant | Loud agonizing door creaks waking up sleeping baby |
| 563 | Home Maintenance | **Drilling through glazed ceramic and porcelain bathroom tiles** | Using standard hammer drill on tiles cracks the entire tile into spiderweb fractures. | Place masking tape over mark, turn OFF hammer mode, use carbide spade tip or diamond core bit | Cracking expensive imported bathroom wall tiles |
| 564 | Furniture & Decor | **Identifying Genuine Top-Grain Leather vs Bonded / PU Faux Leather** | Faux PU leather begins peeling and shedding plastic flakes (delamination) after 2 years. | Check unfinished suede backside, smell for rich earthy leather aroma, check water absorption | Sofa peeling into thousands of black plastic flakes across carpet |
| 565 | Furniture & Decor | **Glass Table Safety: Annealed Float Glass vs Tempered Glass** | Regular annealed glass tables shatter into dagger-sharp deadly shards when broken. | Insist on minimum 10mm-12mm Tempered Safety Glass with polished beveled edges | Catastrophic deep arterial lacerations if someone leans or falls on table |
| 566 | Furniture & Decor | **Hanging heavy 30 kg mirror on hollow brick / drywall wall** | Hanging heavy mirrors with simple picture wire and single nail pulls chunks out of wall. | Use French Cleat aluminum interlocking brackets screwed directly into concrete / wall studs | 30 kg glass mirror crashing down in middle of night |
| 567 | Furniture & Decor | **Wood Polish: French Spirit Shellac Polish vs PU Lacquer Polish** | Traditional French spirit polish gets permanent white water-ring stains from hot tea cups. | Use two-pack Polyurethane (PU) clear coat for dining tables; French polish for antique cabinets | White cloudy cup rings permanently ruining dining table shine |
| 568 | Furniture & Decor | **Rooftop & Balcony Planters: Weight load capacity on RCC cantilevers** | Loading balconies with hundreds of kilos of wet garden soil risks cantilever slab collapse. | Use lightweight potting mix (50% cocopeat + 25% perlite + 25% compost), place heavy pots over beams | Structural cracks appearing along balcony joint line |
| 774 | Electrical & Wiring | **Sizing Electrical Wire Gauge (RM) for AC and Water Geyser Circuits** | Connecting a 2-ton AC to thin 1.5RM lighting wire causes wires to melt inside PVC conduits. | Use minimum 4.0RM copper wire for AC; 2.5RM for geyser; dedicated circuit breaker for each | Electrical wiring fire inside wall conduit burning house down |
| 775 | Electrical & Wiring | **Installing Residual Current Circuit Breaker (RCCB / RCBO 30mA)** | Standard MCB circuit breakers only trip on massive short circuits, not during human electrocution. | Install 30mA sensitivity RCCB in main distribution board; test test-button monthly | Fatal electrocution from touching faulty washing machine or wet wall |
| 776 | Electrical & Wiring | **Installing Surge Protection Device (SPD Type 2) in Distribution Board** | Lightning strikes on nearby utility lines send voltage spikes that destroy all home TVs and ACs. | Install Type-2 DIN-rail SPD connected to solid earth; ensure earth resistance is under 5 Ohms | All home electronics and inverter boards fried in a single thunderstorm |
| 777 | Plumbing & Water | **Sizing Domestic Water Pressure Booster Pump: VFD vs Pressure Tank** | Cheap pressure switch pumps cycle on and off every 3 seconds, causing violent pressure surges. | Choose Variable Frequency Drive (VFD) booster pump for continuous silent constant pressure | Blowing out toilet bidet sprayers and bursting flexible water hoses |
| 778 | Plumbing & Water | **Treating Persistent Wall Efflorescence (Saltpeter / লোনা লাগা)** | Applying wall putty over damp salted bricks results in flakes falling off again in 3 months. | Chip plaster down to bare brick; wash with mild acid; apply waterproof crystalline slurry (e.g. Sika) | Ruined luxury interior paint and crumbling brickwork |
| 779 | Plumbing & Water | **Installing Anti-Syphon Trap (P-Trap) on Bathroom Floor Drains** | Drains without water seal traps allow sewer gases and cockroaches to enter living spaces. | Install deep P-trap with minimum 50mm water seal; ensure regular water flow to prevent dry-out | Horrendous sewer gas smell and cockroach infestation inside bedrooms |
| 780 | Plumbing & Water | **Sizing Rooftop Water Tank Capacity for Multi-Family Apartment** | Undersized tanks run out of water during morning rush hour, burning pump motors dry. | Calculate 150 liters per person per day x total residents + 20% safety storage margin | Building running out of water every morning; burnt water pumps |
| 781 | Safety & Civil | **Installing Carbon Monoxide (CO) Detector near Kitchen and Gas Lines** | Carbon monoxide is invisible, odorless, and kills sleeping families during silent gas leaks. | Install battery-backed digital CO alarm 5 feet off floor near sleeping areas and kitchen | Silent death from carbon monoxide poisoning during sleep |
| 782 | Safety & Civil | **Core-Cutting RCC Beams for Kitchen Hood Ducting: Structural Integrity** | Plumbers blindly drill 6-inch holes through structural beams, cutting through main tension rebar. | Never core-drill through concrete structural beams; drill through non-loadbearing exterior brick walls | Structural weakening and catastrophic beam shear failure |
| 865 | Home Maintenance | **Descaling Espresso Machine and Electric Kettle with Citric Acid** | Using harsh chemical descalers corrodes copper boilers; using vinegar leaves persistent foul odor. | Dissolve 2 tablespoons of food-grade citric acid powder per liter of warm water; flush 3 times with clean water | Boiler heating element burning out due to thick calcium scale |
| 866 | Home Maintenance | **Cleaning Washing Machine Rubber Door Gasket Fungus (Sodium Percarbonate)** | Closing front-load washing machine door after wash breeds black toxic mold inside rubber folds. | Wipe gasket dry after every wash; leave door ajar; run 90 deg C wash cycle with Sodium Percarbonate monthly | Foul musty smell transferring onto all freshly washed clothes |
| 867 | Home Maintenance | **Replacing Toilet Tank Fill Valve and Adjusting Water Float Line** | Water level set above overflow tube causes continuous silent water wastage into toilet bowl. | Adjust float screw so water level stops 1 inch below top of overflow tube; test silent shutoff | Thousands of liters of water wasted and high electricity pumping bills |
| 868 | Home Maintenance | **Unclogging Bathroom Sink Aerator Screen to Restore Water Pressure** | Sediment and pipe rust collect inside tap faucet aerators, reducing water flow to a trickle. | Unscrew aerator mesh counter-clockwise with wrench; soak in warm vinegar to dissolve mineral scale | Mistakenly calling expensive plumber to replace entire faucet fixture |
| 869 | Home Maintenance | **Installing Handheld Bidet Sprayer (Shattaf) with Dedicated Brass Shutoff T-Valve** | Leaving bidet sprayer hose under continuous municipal pressure bursts flexible hose, flooding apartment. | Install dedicated brass shutoff T-valve; close valve after use to relieve hose pressure | Flexible hose bursting at 2 AM, flooding entire apartment with water |
| 870 | Home Maintenance | **Hanging Heavy Ceiling Fan: Fan-Rated Steel Outlet Box vs Light Box** | Mounting ceiling fan on a lightweight plastic light box causes vibrations to rip box from ceiling. | Insist on UL-listed metal Fan-Rated outlet box anchored directly to concrete ceiling rebar | Ceiling fan wobbling violently and falling onto bed or floor |
| 871 | Home Maintenance | **Treating Cockroach Infestation with Indoxacarb Gel Bait (Advion)** | Spraying chemical aerosol insecticides scatters cockroaches into walls, worsening the infestation. | Place pea-sized dots of Indoxacarb gel bait in dark hinge corners, under sinks, and behind fridge | Cockroaches multiplying and contaminating food supplies with bacteria |
| 872 | Home Maintenance | **Treating Bedbug Infestation: Crossfire Insecticide vs Heat Treatment** | Bedbugs have developed genetic resistance to common pyrethroid sprays, making them useless. | Use Clothianidin/Metofluthrin (Crossfire) non-repellent spray + steam clean mattress seams at 60 deg C | Months of sleepless nights, agonizing bites, and throwing away beds |
| 873 | Home Maintenance | **Preventing Pantry Moth (Weevil) Infestation in Grains and Flour** | Storing rice and flour in open paper bags allows moth larvae to spin webs and spoil food. | Store all grains in airtight glass/heavy plastic containers; freeze new grain bags for 48h to kill eggs | Discovering pantry grains infested with crawling white worms |
| 874 | Home Maintenance | **Treating Oil Stains on Concrete Floor with Cat Litter and Degreaser** | Spraying water on fresh engine oil spread stain deeper into porous concrete floor. | Pour clay cat litter over fresh oil, crush with shoes, leave 24h to absorb; wash with heavy degreaser | Permanent dark oil stain permanently ruining garage floor appearance |

| 347 | Guitars & Strings | **Inspecting Acoustic Guitar Action, Fret Buzz, and Truss Rod** | High string action hurts beginner fingers; bowed neck causes unplayable fret buzz. | Check string height at 12th fret (<2.5mm), sight down neck for twist, check two-way truss rod | Giving up guitar within 2 weeks due to excruciating finger pain |
| 348 | Guitars & Strings | **Solid Spruce Top vs Laminated Wood Acoustic Guitar resonance** | Plywood laminate tops sound thin and do not age/improve in tone over time. | Look at soundhole cross-section to verify continuous wood grain across top plate | Paying solid-wood prices for cheap industrial plywood guitar |
| 349 | Guitars & Strings | **Setting up Electric Guitar Intonation and Bridge Saddles** | Chords sound horribly out of tune high up the fretboard even when open strings are in tune. | Tune open string, compare harmonic at 12th fret vs fretted note, adjust saddle screw | Guitar sounding chronically dissonant during soloing |
| 350 | Guitars & Strings | **Single Coil vs Humbucker Pickups for noise-free playing** | Single-coil Strat pickups hum loudly when playing under LED lights or computer monitors. | Choose Humbuckers or HSS configuration for heavy overdrive without 60-cycle hum | Obnoxious buzzing ruining guitar recordings |
| 351 | Guitars & Strings | **Selecting Classical Guitar Nylon Strings vs Steel Strings** | Putting steel strings on a classical nylon guitar snaps the headstock or tears bridge off. | Never put steel strings on classical guitar; check internal bracing | Permanent destruction of instrument wooden soundboard |
| 352 | Instruments | **Digital Piano: Graded Hammer Action vs Synth Semi-Weighted keys** | Springy semi-weighted keyboards ruin classical piano finger technique and dynamics. | Insist on 88-key Graded Hammer Standard (GHS) or Tri-sensor Scaled Hammer action | Inability to develop acoustic piano touch sensitivity |
| 353 | Instruments | **Buying a Beginner Violin: Peg slipping, soundpost alignment, and bridge curve** | Cheap factory violins have untapered friction pegs that slip instantly, detuning strings. | Check ebony pegs fitting, ensure soundpost is standing upright inside body, check bridge arch | Violin physically impossible to tune or play |
| 354 | Instruments | **Electronic Drum Kit: Mesh Heads vs Rubber Pads** | Hard rubber drum pads vibrate wrists painfully and sound like tapping on hard plastic. | Choose dual-zone Mesh Heads for realistic stick rebound and whisper-quiet practice | Wrist tendonitis and neighbor complaints about thumping |
| 355 | Instruments | **Maintaining Acoustic Guitar Humidity (preventing body cracks)** | Dry winter weather or continuous air conditioning dries wood, cracking soundboard. | Keep guitar in case with 2-way soundhole humidifier; maintain 45-55% relative humidity | Soundboard splitting wide open down center seam |
| 356 | Sports Equipment | **Knocking-in and Oiling a Cricket Bat (English vs Kashmir Willow)** | Playing with an un-knocked English willow bat cracks the edges on the very first fast ball. | Apply raw linseed oil, 6 hours of mallet knocking on edges and toe, apply protective scuff sheet | Bat snapping in half during the first club match |
| 357 | Sports Equipment | **Distinguishing English Willow Grade 1 vs Grade 3 vs Kashmir Willow** | Sellers bleach Kashmir willow with chemicals and sell it as premium Grade 1 English Willow. | Check grain count (6-10 straight equidistant grains), natural wood blemishes, bat ping test | Overpaying by 15,000+ BDT for heavy sluggish bat |
| 358 | Sports Equipment | **Selecting Badminton Racket: Head Heavy vs Even Balance & String Tension** | Strung at 28+ lbs tension without proper wrist technique causes tennis elbow and snapped frames. | Beginners should choose 4U Even Balance with 22-24 lbs BG65 string tension | Severe wrist inflammation and shattered racket frame |
| 359 | Sports Equipment | **Choosing Football Boots: Firm Ground (FG) vs Artificial Turf (TF)** | Wearing long FG molded plastic studs on artificial turf fields causes ACL knee tears. | Use TF rubber-nubbed turf shoes on modern 5-a-side artificial turf pitches | Torn knee meniscus or ACL ligament rupture |
| 360 | Sports Equipment | **Home Gym Power Rack Safety: 11-Gauge Steel vs Thin Commercial Tubes** | Thin sheet metal racks buckle when a 100 kg barbell is dropped onto safety pins. | Minimum 2x2 or 3x3 inch 11-gauge (3mm thick) steel tubing with through-bolted safety bars | Barbell crashing onto lifter's chest or neck |
| 361 | Sports Equipment | **Olympic Barbell (2-inch sleeves) vs Standard 1-inch barbell** | Cheap 1-inch screw-collar bars bend permanently when loaded past 80 kg. | Insist on 20 kg 7-foot Olympic barbell with rotating bronze bushings / bearings | Permanent bar bend causing wrist injury during bench press |
| 362 | Outdoor & Trekking | **Selecting Camping Tent: Hydrostatic Head Waterproof Rating** | Cheap single-layer tents with 800mm rating leak like a sieve during tropical downpours. | Double-layer tent with minimum 3000mm hydrostatic head flysheet and bathtub floor | Waking up floating in soaking wet sleeping bag at 2 AM |
| 363 | Outdoor & Trekking | **Backpack Sizing: Torso Length Adjustment and Hip Belt Load Transfer** | Carrying 15 kg on shoulders without hip belt load transfer pinches spinal nerves. | Measure C7 vertebra to iliac crest; adjust internal frame so 80% weight sits on pelvis | Debilitating shoulder spasms and lower back disc compression |
| 364 | Outdoor & Trekking | **Trekking Boot selection: Ankle Support and Vibram Grip** | Wearing running sneakers on slippery muddy trails causes severe ankle sprains. | Mid-cut waterproof breathable boots with deep multidirectional lug outsole | Slipping off mountain ridge or fractured ankle miles from help |
| 365 | Outdoor & Trekking | **Water Purification on Trail: Gravity Filter vs Chlorine Dioxide Tabs** | Drinking clear mountain stream water without filtration causes severe Giardia dysentery. | 0.1-micron hollow-fiber membrane filter (Sawyer Squeeze) plus chemical tabs for viruses | Violent vomiting and dehydration in the wilderness |
| 366 | Outdoor & Trekking | **Selecting Binoculars: Roof Prism vs Porro Prism and Magnification** | High 16x binoculars without tripod shake uncontrollably, giving headaches. | Choose 8x42 or 10x42 Roof Prism with BaK-4 glass and fully multi-coated (FMC) lenses | Dim, blurry double vision with severe chromatic aberration |
| 995 | Musical Accessories | **Selecting Acoustic Guitar Capo: Spring Clamp vs Adjustable Screw Tension** | Strong spring capos apply excessive uneven pressure, pulling all guitar strings sharp out of tune. | Choose an adjustable tension screw capo (e.g. Shubb or G7th) to match fretboard radius perfectly | Guitar sounding hopelessly out of tune whenever capo is attached |

## Banking, Finance & Investments

| ID | Subcategory | Scenario Title | Core Problem / Friction | Critical Checklist | Primary Risk |
| :---: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| 367 | Personal Banking | **Selecting Sanchayapatra (National Savings Certificate) categories** | Investing beyond individual investment limits (e.g. 50 Lakh BDT) triggers automatic forfeiture of profits. | Check Paribar Sanchayapatra, Pensioner, or 3-Month Profit scheme eligibility, e-TIN, NID | Source tax deduction of 10% and profit reversal penalties |
| 368 | Personal Banking | **Opening DPS (Deposit Pension Scheme) and premature closure penalty** | Closing DPS before 1 year forfeits all interest and incurs administrative penalty charges. | Check compound interest calculation, grace period for late monthly installments (usually 30 days) | Losing months of accrued interest on emergency withdrawal |
| 369 | Personal Banking | **Opening a Joint Bank Account: 'Either or Survivor' vs 'Jointly Operated'** | In 'Jointly Operated' accounts, both signatures are required for every single cheque or withdrawal. | Select 'Either or Survivor' clause to allow seamless access if one partner falls ill or passes away | Funds frozen completely if one spouse is incapacitated |
| 370 | Personal Banking | **Handling Dormant / Inactive Bank Account revival** | Accounts with no customer-initiated transaction for 1-2 years are frozen by central bank regulations. | Submit fresh KYC form, biometric verification, small cash deposit or withdrawal at home branch | Cheques bouncing and automated utility auto-debits failing |
| 371 | Personal Banking | **Updating Bank Account Nominee vs Legal Heirs Succession rights** | Under legal precedent in Bangladesh, a nominee is merely a custodian, not the absolute legal owner of funds. | Nominee form with NID and photo; prepare registered will if specific distribution is desired | Bitter court battles between nominee and other family heirs |
| 372 | Cards & Payments | **Avoiding the 'Minimum Amount Due' trap on Credit Cards** | Paying only the minimum due incurs compounding interest of 20-25% on the entire balance from purchase date. | Always pay 'Total Amount Due' before due date; set up auto-debit from salary account | Debt spiraling out of control into hundreds of thousands in interest |
| 373 | Cards & Payments | **Getting Credit Card Annual Fee Waived (14-18 transactions rule)** | Banks quietly charge 3,000-10,000 BDT annual fee unless minimum transaction count is met. | Track monthly POS/online swipes; call call-center immediately when annual fee appears on statement | Paying unnecessary bank charges every year |
| 374 | Cards & Payments | **Filing a Credit Card Chargeback for undelivered online goods** | Banks must initiate Visa/Mastercard dispute resolution if merchant fails to deliver within promised window. | Chargeback dispute form within 45-60 days of transaction, merchant chat logs, invoice | Losing money to fraudulent e-commerce scams |
| 375 | Cards & Payments | **Recovering money sent to wrong bKash / Nagad / Upay account** | Once cashout occurs, MFS providers cannot reverse funds without recipient's formal consent or police GD. | Call MFS helpline immediately to freeze recipient wallet; file police GD at Thana within hours | Permanent loss of transferred money if recipient cashes out |
| 376 | Cards & Payments | **Protecting against MFS (bKash/Nagad) social engineering OTP scams** | Scammers impersonate helpline officers claiming your account is locked and asking for 5-digit PIN/OTP. | Never disclose PIN or SMS OTP to anyone under any circumstances; official staff never ask for PIN | Entire mobile wallet balance drained within 30 seconds |
| 377 | Loans & Debt | **Checking CIB (Credit Information Bureau) report before applying for home loan** | A single forgotten 500 BDT overdue on an old credit card flags you as 'Defaulter' in Bangladesh Bank CIB. | Request self CIB report via Bangladesh Bank portal; clear all legacy unclosed credit cards | Instant rejection of home loan application after paying non-refundable fees |
| 378 | Loans & Debt | **Floating vs Fixed Interest Rate terms in Home Loans** | Banks advertise low 8% teaser rates that jump to 12% after the first year under floating rate clauses. | Check interest rate reset frequency, Bangladesh Bank reference SMART rate spread | Monthly EMI jumping by 25%, breaking family budget |
| 379 | Loans & Debt | **Negotiating Loan Prepayment / Early Settlement Penalty** | Banks charge 1-2% penalty fee if you pay off your loan early using your own savings. | Ensure agreement states zero prepayment penalty from personal income sources | Paying 50,000+ BDT just for the privilege of clearing your own debt early |
| 380 | Loans & Debt | **Applying for SME (Small & Medium Enterprise) loan from commercial banks** | Banks reject 80% of SME loans due to lack of audited cash flow records and trade license tenure. | Minimum 2-3 years trade license, daily sales ledger (khatiyan), VAT returns, collateral valuation | Desperate borrowing from loan sharks at 10% monthly interest |
| 381 | Loans & Debt | **Drafting a legally binding Promissory Note / IOU for personal lending** | Lending money to friends/relatives on verbal promises leaves zero legal evidence for recovery. | Special revenue stamp paper (300 BDT), 2 independent witnesses, signed bank cross-cheque | Total loss of lent money and destroyed relationships |
| 382 | Investments | **Opening a BO (Beneficiary Owner) Account for Stock Market** | Unregistered sub-brokerage accounts expose investors to broker portfolio mismanagement. | CDBL-registered brokerage firm, bank account statement, routing number, nominee NID | Broker unauthorized trading or failure to credit dividends |
| 383 | Investments | **Applying for Initial Public Offerings (IPO) through Pro-Rata allocation** | Investors must maintain minimum secondary market portfolio equity to qualify for IPO quotas. | Verify Bangladesh Securities and Exchange Commission (BSEC) minimum equity threshold (e.g. 50,000 BDT) | IPO application rejected and application fee forfeited |
| 384 | Investments | **Spotting MLM / Ponzi investment scams (e.g. MTFE / E-commerce cashback)** | Any scheme promising 'guaranteed 20-30% monthly return' is a mathematical Ponzi fraud. | Check BSEC license; verify company does not require recruiting friends to unlock earnings | Losing entire family life savings and community ostracization |
| 385 | Investments | **Investing in Treasury Bonds via Bangladesh Bank MI Module** | Long-term sovereign government bonds provide risk-free guaranteed yields with zero default risk. | Open Business Partner (BP) ID through primary dealer bank, participate in central bank auctions | Missing out on safe 11-12% sovereign yields |
| 386 | Investments | **Evaluating Mutual Funds: Open-Ended vs Closed-Ended Funds** | Closed-ended mutual funds often trade at a steep 30-40% discount to their Net Asset Value (NAV). | Check Asset Management Company (AMC) track record, portfolio disclosure, expense ratio (<2%) | Capital erosion due to high fund manager fees and low returns |
| 455 | Loans | **Home loan balance transfer to another bank with lower interest rate** | Processing fees, valuation fees, and mortgage stamp duties at the new bank often outweigh interest savings. | Calculate Net Present Value (NPV) of interest difference minus all closing charges; check prepayment penalty | Losing money on fees despite lower advertised percentage |
| 456 | Loans | **Car financing: Flat interest rate vs Reducing balance interest rate calculation** | A flat 7% rate is equivalent to an actual reducing rate of nearly 13%, doubling actual interest paid. | Demand reducing balance amortization schedule table; calculate true Effective Annual Rate (EAR) | Paying double the expected interest on auto loan |
| 457 | Loans | **Applying for Student Education Loan for overseas studies** | Banks require tangible property collateral worth 1.5x to 2x the loan amount and a co-borrower. | Unencumbered property deed, non-encumbrance certificate, university admission I-20/CAS letter | Loan processing delayed past university payment deadline |
| 458 | Loans | **Borrowing against Fixed Deposit (OD against FDR) for emergency cash** | Allows borrowing up to 90% of FDR value at 1-2% above deposit rate without breaking the FDR. | FDR lien marking letter, overdraft account setup, understanding interest-only monthly service | Breaking long-term FDR prematurely and forfeiting compound interest |
| 459 | Loans | **Handling guarantor liability when the primary borrower defaults on loan** | Signing as loan guarantor makes you equally liable under Artha Rin Adalat Ain; banks can seize guarantor assets. | Never sign as guarantor unless prepared to pay debt; check borrower creditworthiness | Guarantor bank accounts frozen and property auctioned by court |
| 460 | Loans | **Settling a defaulted bank loan through One-Time Settlement (OTS)** | Banks can waive compounding interest and penal charges under central bank circulars if lump sum is paid. | Submit formal OTS proposal to bank Board of Directors citing financial distress, demand written settlement letter | Paying cash without board approval and remaining classified as defaulter |
| 461 | Loans | **Microfinance NGO loans (e.g. Grameen, BRAC) weekly repayment dynamics** | Weekly installment pressure in rural areas leads to taking second loans from loan sharks (overlapping loans). | Calculate actual annual APR (often 24%+); ensure income generation matches weekly repayment frequency | Falling into multi-NGO debt spiral and asset distress sale |
| 462 | Loans | **Agriculture credit / Krishi Loan interest subsidy eligibility** | Bangladesh Bank mandates 4% subsidized interest rate for pulse, oilseed, and spice cultivation. | Krishi Card (কৃষি কার্ড), land holding proof, crop cultivation verification from Upazila Agriculture Officer | Overpaying standard commercial loan rates for farming |
| 463 | Loans | **Export Development Fund (EDF) financing for manufacturing exporters** | Provides foreign currency financing at SOFR+spread for importing raw materials for export. | Confirmed export master LC, utilization declaration (UD), central bank EDF allocation quota | Defaulting on foreign currency obligations when export proceeds are delayed |
| 464 | Loans | **Gold Loan / Pledging jewelry for immediate business capital** | Pawnshops and non-bank financiers undervalue gold purity and charge exorbitant hidden appraisal fees. | Get certified karat purity test (KDM 22K), demand written pledge receipt with exact gram weight | Forfeiting family heirloom gold on minor payment delay |
| 465 | Cards & Digital | **Disputing ATM cash dispensation failure when account is debited** | ATM machine counts money but fails to dispense cash while sending debit SMS to phone. | Preserve ATM error slip, note exact ATM machine ID, lodge formal dispute within 24 hours with issuer | Losing funds permanently if ATM electronic journal log expires |
| 466 | Cards & Digital | **International Credit Card dynamic currency conversion (DCC) markup trap** | Paying in local BDT at foreign POS terminals incurs double conversion fees (up to 8% markup). | Always choose to pay in the foreign local currency (USD, EUR, GBP) at POS counter; never BDT | Paying 5-8% extra on every foreign hotel and shopping bill |
| 467 | Cards & Digital | **Credit card reward points expiration and redemption value calculation** | Points expire after 24 months or yield terrible value when redeemed for catalog merchandise. | Redeem points directly against annual fee waiver or airline miles for highest conversion ratio | Accumulated 50,000 points expiring into zero value |
| 468 | Cards & Digital | **Setting daily transaction limits on mobile banking apps to prevent drain** | Default high daily transfer limits allow phone thieves to empty entire savings. | Lower daily ATM, POS, and online transaction limits via mobile banking app settings | Entire bank balance stolen in a single fraudulent transaction |
| 469 | Cards & Digital | **Virtual Credit Cards (VCC) for recurring international SaaS subscriptions** | Free trial subscriptions silently convert to annual recurring charges that are hard to cancel. | Use disposable virtual cards with strict spending limits (e.g. 5 USD) for software trials | Surprise $300 annual renewal charges on personal credit card |
| 470 | Investments | **Buying 22K vs 24K Hallmarked Gold jewelry vs Bullion bars** | Jewelers deduct 15-20% making charges and wastage charges upon resale of ornamental gold. | Buy standardized certified 24K bullion minted bars with Assay certificate for investment | Losing 25% of invested capital immediately on jewelry resale |
| 471 | Investments | **Opening a Foreign Currency (FC) account for export/remittance retention** | Allows holding USD/EUR balances hedge against local currency inflation and devaluation. | Export proceeds certificate, freelancer tax-exemption certificate, bank FC account opening form | Mandatory automatic conversion to local currency at unfavorable rates |
| 472 | Investments | **Evaluating Real Estate Investment Trusts (REITs) vs physical flat purchase** | Physical flats yield 3-4% rental return with tenant headache; REITs offer liquidity and diversification. | Compare net dividend yield, underlying commercial tenant lease profile, liquidity on stock exchange | Locking capital in illiquid flat that takes 2 years to sell |
| 473 | Investments | **Understanding Stock Market Circuit Breakers and Floor Price rules** | Regulatory floor prices freeze trading volume, making it impossible to exit falling stocks. | Monitor daily market depth, avoid illiquid small-cap Z-category junk stocks | Holding frozen shares for months while company fundamentals collapse |
| 474 | Investments | **Spotting insider trading and price manipulation in penny stocks** | Telegram and Facebook cartels pump dead company shares before dumping them on retail public. | Check audited EPS, P/E ratio, director shareholding patterns, avoid viral social media stock tips | Buying at the absolute top right before the dump; 80% capital loss |
| 475 | Investments | **Dividend Income Taxation: 10% vs 15% withholding tax based on e-TIN** | Failing to submit updated e-TIN to brokerage firm results in maximum 15% source tax deduction. | Update e-TIN in BO account profile through DP (Depository Participant) before record date | Overpaying withholding tax on annual stock dividends |
| 476 | Investments | **Investing in Shariah-Compliant Sukuk (Islamic Bonds)** | Sukuk represent asset-backed ownership rather than debt, offering regular rental yields. | Review Shariah supervisory board fatwa, underlying tangible asset verification, credit rating | Misunderstanding risk profile and capital redemption terms |
| 477 | Investments | **Venture Capital & Angel Investing in startups: SAFE notes vs Convertible Debt** | Angel investors without liquidation preference get wiped out in subsequent down-rounds. | Simple Agreement for Future Equity (SAFE) with valuation cap, pro-rata rights, 1x non-participating liquidation preference | Total loss of investment when startup raises dilutive round |
| 478 | Investments | **Hedging against inflation using sovereign inflation-indexed instruments** | Keeping cash in regular savings accounts guarantees negative real returns after inflation. | Allocate across inflation-beating assets: high-yield sovereign savings, land, diversified equities | Real purchasing power halving over 7 years in cash savings |
| 479 | Investments | **Setting up an Emergency Rainy-Day Fund: Sizing and Liquidity** | Storing emergency funds in 5-year FDR leaves you helpless during medical crises. | Keep 6 months of essential living expenses in high-yield liquid money-market savings or call accounts | Forced distress sale of assets or borrowing from loan sharks |
| 480 | Business Finance | **Letter of Credit (LC): Sight LC vs Usance / Deferred LC risks** | In Usance LC, the buyer receives goods but must pay after 90/180 days; currency fluctuations can destroy margins. | Hedge exchange rate exposure with forward contract; verify issuing bank international credit rating | Incurring massive foreign exchange loss on currency depreciation |
| 481 | Business Finance | **Understanding Discrepant LC Documents and Bank Rejection** | A tiny typographic discrepancy between commercial invoice and bill of lading halts payment. | Follow UCP 600 rules strictly; match commodity description word-for-word against LC terms | Goods stranded at destination port; buyer demanding price discounts |
| 482 | Business Finance | **Bank Guarantee (BG): Performance Bond vs Advance Payment Guarantee** | An unconditional bank guarantee can be encashed by the client on simple demand without proving default. | Ensure conditional trigger terms; negotiate expiry date clause and return of original BG instrument | Client encashing millions in bank guarantee during contractual disputes |
| 483 | Business Finance | **Factoring and Invoice Discounting for corporate suppliers** | Waiting 90-120 days for multinational corporate clients to pay invoices cripples business cash flow. | Partner with licensed factoring company; submit verified purchase orders for 80% immediate liquidity | Insolvency and inability to pay supplier wages while awaiting receivables |
| 484 | Business Finance | **Setting up Cash Credit (Hypothecation - CC Hypo) working capital loan** | Banks conduct unannounced physical audits of warehouse stock pledged under hypothecation. | Maintain daily stock register (Drawing Power calculation), keep stock insured against fire/flood | Bank freezing CC limit and filing loan recovery lawsuit for stock discrepancies |
| 875 | Commercial Banking | **Opening High-Yield Business Money Market Account for Cash Reserves** | Leaving operational cash in 0% checking accounts loses thousands to annual inflation. | Negotiate sweep accounts that sweep overnight balances into short-term treasury repos | Losing substantial interest income on corporate cash reserves |
| 876 | Commercial Banking | **Enrolling in Bank Automated Bill Pay with Overdraft Protection** | Setting auto-pay on utility bills without low-balance alerts triggers compounding overdraft fees. | Link secondary savings account as automatic backup buffer; set SMS balance alerts at 5,000 BDT | Paying 500 BDT overdraft penalty on a 200 BDT automated bill |
| 877 | Commercial Banking | **Equipment Lease Financing vs Commercial Bank Loan for Machinery** | Direct loans require 30% upfront cash downpayment, depleting company working capital reserves. | Evaluate operating lease for off-balance sheet tax deduction vs capital lease for ownership | Depleting emergency business reserves to purchase depreciating machines |
| 878 | Commercial Banking | **Negotiating Merchant Credit Card Interchange Fees with Acquirer Bank** | Banks quietly charge small merchants 2.5-3.5% transaction fees on standard debit card swipes. | Request interchange-plus pricing model; negotiate lower fee brackets based on monthly turnover | Losing 3% of top-line retail revenue purely to bank card fees |

| 880 | Commercial Banking | **Filing Zero VAT Returns (Mushak 9.1) for Inactive Corporate Entities** | Failing to submit VAT return even with zero commercial sales attracts automated 10,000 BDT fine. | Submit zero return on NBR VAT portal before 15th of every month without fail | Accumulated statutory penalties and frozen business tax identity |
| 881 | Commercial Banking | **Managing Employee Tax Deducted at Source (TDS on Salary)** | Failing to deposit deducted employee income tax to government treasury attracts 2% monthly interest penalty. | Deposit TDS monthly via A-Challan; submit annual Form 108 salary statement to tax commissioner | Company directors held personally liable for tax recovery |

| 884 | Commercial Banking | **Claiming Government Cash Export Incentive (রপ্তানি নগদ প্রণোদনা)** | Submitting incentive applications past the 180-day window forfeits the 4-10% cash incentive. | Submit proceeds realization certificate (PRC), BGMEA/BKMEA audit certificate within 180 days | Forfeiting millions of BDT in legitimate government export subsidies |

| 938 | Commercial Trade | **Setting up Offshore Multi-Currency Business Account (Wise / Airwallex)** | Traditional banks charge predatory 4-5% wire spreads on international IT consulting payments. | Submit company incorporation papers, utility bill, and website contract proof for multi-currency routing | Losing thousands of USD every month to commercial bank currency margins |
| 939 | Commercial Trade | **Registering Trademark under Madrid Protocol for Multi-Country Protection** | Filing separate trademark applications in 20 countries costs tens of thousands in foreign lawyer fees. | File single international application through WIPO Madrid System based on home trademark filing | Rival registering your brand name in export markets, locking you out |
| 940 | Commercial Trade | **Setting up Recognized Employee Provident Fund (PF) Trustee Board** | Unrecognized provident funds are taxed as standard corporate income, losing tax benefits. | Draft PF Trust Deed, appoint equal employee-employer trustees, obtain NBR Commissioner recognition | Tax authorities seizing provident fund balances as corporate taxable income |

| 944 | Commercial Trade | **Setting up Executive Performance Bonus with Legal Clawback Clauses** | Executives manipulate short-term quarterly metrics to claim bonuses right before company crashes. | Draft bonus agreement with mandatory 3-year clawback clause for financial restatements or fraud | Inability to recover millions in fraudulently claimed executive bonuses |

| 387 | Police & Citizen Rights | **Your legal rights when stopped and searched by police at night** | Police officers in plainclothes cannot search citizens without showing official identification badge. | Ask for official police ID, demand uniform presence, remain polite, never run or argue aggressively | Arbitrary detention or fabricated charges |
| 388 | Police & Citizen Rights | **Filing an Online GD (General Diary) for lost documents / threats** | Filing an online GD provides instant digital tracking number recognized by all authorities. | Online GD app / portal, NID verification, exact time/place of occurrence, serial numbers of lost items | Going to Thana in person and facing harassment by duty officer |
| 389 | Police & Citizen Rights | **Difference between General Diary (GD) and First Information Report (FIR)** | A GD is an informational record, whereas an FIR triggers mandatory police criminal investigation and arrest powers. | Cognizable offense (FIR required) vs non-cognizable (GD recorded); get signed carbon copy of FIR | Police refusing to register FIR for cognizable crimes like robbery |
| 390 | Police & Citizen Rights | **What to do if named falsely in a police criminal case / FIR** | Hiding or running away leads to arrest warrant and attachment of movable property (ক্রোক আদেশ). | Obtain certified copy of FIR, engage High Court / Sessions lawyer immediately for Anticipatory Bail | Arrest and spending months in jail awaiting trial |
| 391 | Police & Citizen Rights | **Securing Anticipatory Bail (আগাম জামিন) from High Court Division** | Anticipatory bail protects an innocent citizen from arrest before formal police charge-sheet is filed. | File application under Section 498 CrPC in High Court Division, surrender physically before court bench | Sudden nighttime arrest by law enforcement |
| 392 | Litigation & Courts | **Defending against a Cheque Bounce Case (Section 138 Negotiable Instruments Act)** | The complainant must serve a 30-day statutory legal notice within 30 days of dishonor. | Check whether legal notice was received within statutory window, prove security cheque defense | Imprisonment up to 1 year and penalty equal to double the cheque amount |
| 393 | Litigation & Courts | **Filing a Complaint with Directorate of National Consumer Rights Protection (DNCRP)** | Consumers who successfully report fraud or overcharging receive 25% of the penalty fine as cash reward. | Submit complaint on dncrp.portal.gov.bd within 30 days of purchase, attach purchase invoice and photos | Shops continuing to scam customers with impunity |
| 394 | Litigation & Courts | **Responding to a formal Legal Notice sent by an advocate** | Ignoring a legal notice allows the opponent to claim in court that you admitted all allegations. | Consult a qualified lawyer, draft formal point-by-point reply within the stipulated deadline (usually 15 days) | Weakening legal position in subsequent lawsuit |
| 395 | Litigation & Courts | **Filing a Money Suit for recovery of unpaid business debts** | Money suits have strict 3-year limitation period from date of payment default under Limitation Act. | Work orders, delivery challans, invoices, bank statements, demand notice, court fees calculation | Claim becoming time-barred, forfeiting entire debt permanently |
| 396 | Litigation & Courts | **Defending against Section 107 / 117 CrPC peace breach proceedings** | Rivals file false Section 107 cases before Executive Magistrate to intimidate and harass. | Appear before Executive Magistrate with counsel, submit written statement, furnish interim surety bond | Arrest warrant issued by Executive Magistrate |
| 397 | Family Law | **Filing for Dower (Dain-Meher) and Maintenance in Family Court** | A divorced or separated wife can legally claim full prompt dower and child maintenance. | File suit under Family Courts Act 2023, registered Kabin-nama, proof of husband's income | Wife left destitute without financial maintenance for herself and child |
| 398 | Family Law | **Securing Child Custody (Hizanat) in Family Court** | Under welfare of minor principle, court prioritizes child's best interests over parental claims. | Mother has custody rights up to 7 years (boy) or puberty (girl); apply for interim visiting rights | Forced separation from child by in-laws |
| 399 | Family Law | **Obtaining Protection Order under Domestic Violence Act** | Magistrate can pass emergency protection orders prohibiting abuser from entering shared residence. | File application before Judicial Magistrate under Domestic Violence (Prevention and Protection) Act | Continued physical abuse and violent assault |

| 401 | Family Law | **Accessing Government Legal Aid (National Legal Aid Services Organization - NLASO)** | Indigent citizens earning below poverty threshold qualify for 100% free legal representation. | Apply to District Legal Aid Committee at District Judge Court, submit income certificate | Inability to fight court cases due to high lawyer fees |
| 485 | Criminal Procedure | **Arrest without warrant under Section 54 of CrPC (Blast Guidelines)** | Police cannot arbitrarily arrest citizens without identifying themselves and notifying family within 12 hours. | Demand to see police officer rank/badge, insist on notification of family, record time of detention | Enforced disappearance or prolonged illegal detention |
| 486 | Criminal Procedure | **Filing a Naraji (নারাজি দরখাস্ত) Petition against police final report** | When corrupt investigating officer submits final report exonerating genuine criminals. | File protest petition before Magistrate within 15 days; demand further investigation by PBI / CID | Criminal case dismissed permanently due to flawed police report |
| 487 | Criminal Procedure | **Appearing in Court for 164 Confession statement voluntariness** | Police torture suspects into signing fake confessions; Magistrate must give 3 hours reflection time without police presence. | Tell Magistrate about police torture; show physical marks; refuse to make involuntary statements | Conviction based entirely on fabricated confession |
| 488 | Criminal Procedure | **Handling Police Remand (রিমান্ড) hearing before Magistrate** | Defense lawyer must argue that interrogation can be completed at jail gate without physical remand. | Medical fitness examination before and after remand; lawyer opposing remand petition | Severe custodial torture in police custody |
| 489 | Criminal Procedure | **Applying for Bail in Non-Bailable Offenses (Section 497 CrPC)** | Bail is judicial discretion; sickness, infirmity, womanhood, or minor age provide strong statutory grounds. | Medical records from Civil Surgeon, proof of permanent residence, no flight risk affidavit | Prolonged incarceration for years as an undertrial prisoner |
| 490 | Criminal Procedure | **Quashing frivolous criminal proceedings under Section 561A CrPC** | High Court Division has inherent power to quash malicious, fake criminal cases to prevent abuse of court process. | Certified copy of FIR and charge-sheet; prove civil dispute was maliciously converted to criminal case | Enduring years of exhausting criminal trial for a civil business dispute |
| 491 | Criminal Procedure | **Filing an Extortion (চাঁদাবাজি - Section 385 Penal Code) complaint** | Local political cadres demanding monthly protection money from new construction sites or businesses. | Record audio/call logs of extortion demands, CCTV footage, file formal complaint before Chief Judicial Magistrate | Physical assault or forced closure of business |

| 493 | Civil Litigation | **Obtaining Temporary Injunction (অস্থায়ী নিষেধাজ্ঞা - Order 39 CPC)** | Stops opponents from constructing buildings, cutting trees, or selling disputed land during trial. | Prove: 1. Prima facie case, 2. Irreparable injury, 3. Balance of convenience; file urgent petition | Opponent building multi-story structure on your land during lawsuit |
| 494 | Civil Litigation | **Filing Writ of Mandamus in High Court against government inaction** | Compelling a government body (e.g. RAJUK, Land Ministry, Police) to perform mandatory statutory duties. | Serve demand for justice notice; file writ petition under Article 102 of Constitution | Government officials ignoring lawful citizen applications indefinitely |
| 495 | Civil Litigation | **Filing Writ of Habeas Corpus for unlawfully detained family member** | Compels law enforcement agencies to produce an illegally detained citizen before High Court within 24 hours. | Emergency writ petition before High Court Vacation/Regular Bench; sworn affidavit by relative | Detainee remaining in secret unlawful custody |

| 498 | Civil Litigation | **Challenging ex-parte decree (একতরফা ডিক্রি রদ - Order 9 Rule 13 CPC)** | When opponent fraudulently shows fake summons delivery and wins case without your knowledge. | File application within 30 days of knowledge; prove process server never delivered court summons | Losing property without ever knowing a lawsuit existed |
